length units Absolute length units represent a physical measurement when the physical properties of the output medium are known, such as for print layout. This is done by anchoring one of the units to a physical unit or the visual angle unit and then defining the others relative to it. Physical units include cm , in , mm , pc , pt , px , and Q .The anchoring is done differently for low-resolution devices, such as screens, versus high-resolution devices, such as printers.
